FORM SET_CONTROL_PARAMETERS CHANGING PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
                                      TYPE SSFCTRLOP.

  PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_DIALOG  = 'X'.       "출력화면 안보인다.
  PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-PREVIEW    = 'X'.       "인쇄 미리보기

ENDFORM. " SET_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_VBPA_ADRNR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_VBPA_ADRNR USING P_VBELN
                     CHANGING P_ADRNR.
  CLEAR: P_ADRNR.
  SELECT SINGLE ADRNR
    INTO P_ADRNR
    FROM VBPA
   WHERE VBELN EQ P_VBELN
     AND POSNR EQ SPACE
     AND PARVW EQ 'WE'.

ENDFORM. " GET_VBPA_ADRNR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ADRC_TEL_NUMBER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ADRC_TEL_NUMBER USING P_ADRNR
                          CHANGING P_TEL_NUMBER
                                   P_NAME1.

* 2013.03.02 납품처명에 city1로 대체
  CLEAR: P_TEL_NUMBER, P_NAME1.
  CHECK P_ADRNR IS NOT INITIAL.
  SELECT SINGLE TEL_NUMBER CITY1
    INTO (P_TEL_NUMBER, P_NAME1)
    FROM ADRC
   WHERE ADDRNUMBER EQ P_ADRNR.

ENDFORM. " GET_ADRC_TEL_NUMBER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_TVST_ADRNR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       출하지점 주소/전화번호
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_TVST_ADRNR USING P_VSTEL
                     CHANGING P_ADRNR.

  CLEAR: P_ADRNR.

  SELECT SINGLE ADRNR
    INTO P_ADRNR
    FROM TVST
   WHERE VSTEL EQ P_VSTEL.

ENDFORM. " GET_TVST_ADRNR

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ADRC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       주소/전화번호
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ADRC USING P_ADRNR
               CHANGING P_TEL_NUMBER
                        P_CITY1.

  CLEAR: P_TEL_NUMBER, P_CITY1.
  CHECK P_ADRNR IS NOT INITIAL.
  SELECT SINGLE TEL_NUMBER CITY1
    INTO (P_TEL_NUMBER, P_CITY1)
    FROM ADRC
   WHERE ADDRNUMBER EQ P_ADRNR.

ENDFORM. " GET_ADRC

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_0100_1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       출고의뢰서
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_0100_1 USING P_TABIX
                            P_ROWCNT
                            PT_ZSDS0020           TYPE ZSDTY0020
                            PT_ZSDS0021           TYPE ZSDTY0021
                            PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP
                            PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP.

  DATA: LT_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDTY0021,
*s 2014.07.18
        LS_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDS0021,
*2013/04/30
        LT_ZSDS0020   TYPE ZSDTY0020,
        LS_ZSDS0020   TYPE ZSDS0020,
        LT_ZSDS0021_1 TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021_1 TYPE ZSDS0021,
        LT_ZSDS0021_2 TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021_2 TYPE ZSDS0021,
        LV_LOOPCNT    TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOTCNT1    TYPE INT4,
        LV_FRIDX1     TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOIDX1     TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES1     TYPE DEC5_2,
        LV_MOD1       TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES2     TYPE DEC5_2,

        LV_FM_NAME    TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_TOTCNT     TYPE INT4,
        LV_FRIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES      TYPE DEC5_2,
        LV_MOD        TYPE INT4,
        LS_DATA_0100   LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.


* Data 2개 Internal Table로 이동
  CLEAR LV_LOOPCNT.
  LOOP AT PT_ZSDS0020 INTO LS_ZSDS0020.
    ADD  1  TO  LV_LOOPCNT.
    LOOP AT PT_ZSDS0021 INTO LS_ZSDS0021 WHERE VBELN = LS_ZSDS0020-VBELN
    .
*s 2014.07.18
      PERFORM GET_MVGR3_TEXT1 USING  LS_ZSDS0021-MVGR3
                              CHANGING  LS_ZSDS0021-BEZEI.
*e 2014.07.18
      IF LV_LOOPCNT = 1.
        LS_ZSDS0021_1 = LS_ZSDS0021.
        APPEND LS_ZSDS0021_1 TO LT_ZSDS0021_1.
      ELSE.
        LS_ZSDS0021_2 = LS_ZSDS0021.
        APPEND LS_ZSDS0021_2 TO LT_ZSDS0021_2.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
* 스마트폼 함수명 조회
*  PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_1'
  IF SY-UNAME = 'HSD48'.

    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_6_B'
                                 CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
  ELSE.

    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_6'
                                 CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
  ENDIF.


* 스마트폼 호출 1페이지에 12행
  LV_TOTCNT = LINES( LT_ZSDS0021_1 ).
  LV_TOTCNT1 = LINES( LT_ZSDS0021_2 ).
* 12건씩 출력.
  LV_TIMES = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT / 12 ).
  LV_TIMES1 = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT1 / 12 ).
* 나머지
  LV_MOD   = LV_TOTCNT MOD 12.
  LV_MOD1   = LV_TOTCNT1 MOD 12.
* 나머지가 있으면 나머지 전송하기 위해 1을 더한다.
  IF LV_MOD GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES.
  ENDIF.
  IF LV_MOD1 GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES1.
  ENDIF.
  IF LV_TIMES >= LV_TIMES1.
    LV_TIMES2 = LV_TIMES.
  ELSE.
    LV_TIMES2 = LV_TIMES1.
  ENDIF.
* From Index
  LV_FRIDX = 1.
  LV_FRIDX1 = 1.
* 12건씩 출력.
  DO LV_TIMES2 TIMES.
    CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0021.
*   미리보기 설정
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
*   첫페이지
*    IF P_TABIX EQ 1 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
    IF P_TABIX < 3 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   마지막페이지
    IF P_TABIX EQ P_ROWCNT AND SY-INDEX EQ LV_TIMES2.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   To Index
    ADD 12 TO LV_TOIDX.
    IF LV_TOIDX GT LV_TOTCNT.
      LV_TOIDX = LV_TOTCNT.
    ENDIF.
    ADD 12 TO LV_TOIDX1.
    IF LV_TOIDX1 GT LV_TOTCNT1.
      LV_TOIDX1 = LV_TOTCNT1.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LINES OF LT_ZSDS0021_1 FROM LV_FRIDX TO LV_TOIDX TO
    LT_ZSDS0021.
    ADD 12 TO LV_FRIDX.
    IF  LV_TOIDX1 NE 0.
      APPEND LINES OF LT_ZSDS0021_2 FROM LV_FRIDX1 TO LV_TOIDX1 TO
      LT_ZSDS0021.
      ADD 12 TO LV_FRIDX1.
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDDO.

** 스마트폼 호출 1페이지에 12행
*  LV_TOTCNT = LINES( PT_ZSDS0021 ).
** 12건씩 출력.
*  LV_TIMES = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT / 12 ).
** 나머지
*  LV_MOD   = LV_TOTCNT MOD 12.
** 나머지가 있으면 나머지 전송하기 위해 1을 더한다.
*  IF LV_MOD GT 0.
*    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES.
*  ENDIF.
** From Index
*  LV_FRIDX = 1.
** 12건씩 출력.
*  DO LV_TIMES TIMES.
*    CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0021.
**   미리보기 설정
*    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
*    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
**   첫페이지
**    IF P_TABIX EQ 1 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
*    IF P_TABIX < 3 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
*      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = ''.
*    ENDIF.
*
**   마지막페이지
*    IF P_TABIX EQ P_ROWCNT AND SY-INDEX EQ LV_TIMES.
*      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = ''.
*    ENDIF.
*
**   To Index
*    ADD 12 TO LV_TOIDX.
*    IF LV_TOIDX GT LV_TOTCNT.
*      LV_TOIDX = LV_TOTCNT.
*    ENDIF.
*
*APPEND LINES OF PT_ZSDS0021 FROM LV_FRIDX TO LV_TOIDX TO LT_ZSDS0021.
*    ADD 12 TO LV_FRIDX.
*
*    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
*      EXPORTING
*        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
*        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
*      TABLES
*        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
*        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
*      EXCEPTIONS
*        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
*        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
*        SEND_ERROR         = 3
*        USER_CANCELED      = 4
*        OTHERS             = 5.
*
*    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
*      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDDO.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_0100_1

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_0100_2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       거래명세표
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_0100_2 USING P_TABIX
                            P_ROWCNT
                            P_STAMP
                            PT_ZSDS0020           TYPE ZSDTY0020
                            PT_ZSDS0021           TYPE ZSDTY0021
                            PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP
                            PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP.

  DATA: LT_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDS0021,
        LV_FM_NAME    TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_TOTCNT     TYPE INT4,
        LV_FRIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES      TYPE DEC5_2,
        LV_MOD        TYPE INT4,
*단가/금액 선택폼(2013/05/06)
        LV_FORMNAME   TYPE TDSFNAME.

*단가/금액 출력여부(2013/05/06)
  IF  GV_PRINT  = 'X'.
    LV_FORMNAME = 'ZSDSF0020_7'.
  ELSE.
    LV_FORMNAME = 'ZSDSF0020_2'.
  ENDIF.
* 스마트폼 함수명 조회
  PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING LV_FORMNAME
                                CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
*  PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_2'
*                                CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
* 스마트폼 호출 1페이지에 12행
  LV_TOTCNT = LINES( PT_ZSDS0021 ).
* 12건씩 출력.
  LV_TIMES = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT / 12 ).
* 나머지
  LV_MOD   = LV_TOTCNT MOD 12.
* 나머지가 있으면 나머지 전송하기 위해 1을 더한다.
  IF LV_MOD GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES.
  ENDIF.
* From Index
  LV_FRIDX = 1.
* 12건씩 출력.
  DO LV_TIMES TIMES.
    CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0021.
*   미리보기 설정
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
*   첫페이지
    IF P_TABIX EQ 1 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   마지막페이지
    IF P_TABIX EQ P_ROWCNT AND SY-INDEX EQ LV_TIMES.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   To Index
    ADD 12 TO LV_TOIDX.
    IF LV_TOIDX GT LV_TOTCNT.
      LV_TOIDX = LV_TOTCNT.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LINES OF PT_ZSDS0021 FROM LV_FRIDX TO LV_TOIDX TO LT_ZSDS0021
    .
    ADD 12 TO LV_FRIDX.

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
        I_STAMP            = P_STAMP
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDDO.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_0100_2

*&      Form  PRINT_0100_3
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       출고의뢰서, 반출증, CMTR의뢰서
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_0100_3 USING PV_TABINX
                            PV_ROWCNT
                            PT_ZSDS0020           TYPE ZSDTY0020
                            PT_ZSDS0021           TYPE ZSDTY0021
                            PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP
                            PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP.

  DATA: LT_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDS0021,
        LV_FM_NAME    TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_TOTCNT     TYPE INT4,
        LV_FRIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES      TYPE DEC5_2,
        LV_MOD        TYPE INT4,
        LV_INDEX      TYPE INT4.

* 스마트폼 호출 1페이지에 12행
  LV_TOTCNT = LINES( PT_ZSDS0021 ).
* 12건씩 출력.
  LV_TIMES = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT / 12 ).
* 나머지
  LV_MOD   = LV_TOTCNT MOD 12.
* 나머지가 있으면 나머지 전송하기 위해 1을 더한다.
  IF LV_MOD GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES.
  ENDIF.
* From Index
  LV_FRIDX = 1.
* 12건씩 출력.
  DO LV_TIMES TIMES.
    CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0021.
    ADD 1 TO LV_INDEX.
*   미리보기 설정
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
*   첫페이지
    IF PV_TABINX EQ 1 AND LV_INDEX EQ 1.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   To Index
    ADD 12 TO LV_TOIDX.
    IF LV_TOIDX GT LV_TOTCNT.
      LV_TOIDX = LV_TOTCNT.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LINES OF PT_ZSDS0021 FROM LV_FRIDX TO LV_TOIDX TO LT_ZSDS0021
    .
    ADD 12 TO LV_FRIDX.

*   출고의뢰서 %%%%
    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_1'
                                  CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.
*   반출증
    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_4'
                                  CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.

    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.

*%%%%%%%%%%%

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.

*   CMTR의뢰서
    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_3'
                                  CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
*   마지막페이지
    IF PV_TABINX EQ PV_ROWCNT AND LV_INDEX EQ LV_TIMES.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = ''.
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.

  ENDDO.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_0100_3
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_0100_1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       출고의뢰서
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_0100_C USING P_TABIX
                            P_ROWCNT
                            PT_ZSDS0020           TYPE ZSDTY0020
                            PT_ZSDS0021           TYPE ZSDTY0021
                            PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP
                            PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP.

  DATA: LT_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDS0021,
*2013/04/30
        LT_ZSDS0020   TYPE ZSDTY0020,
        LS_ZSDS0020   TYPE ZSDS0020,
        LT_ZSDS0021_1 TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021_1 TYPE ZSDS0021,
        LT_ZSDS0021_2 TYPE ZSDTY0021,
        LS_ZSDS0021_2 TYPE ZSDS0021,
        LV_LOOPCNT    TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOTCNT1    TYPE INT4,
        LV_FRIDX1     TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOIDX1     TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES1     TYPE DEC5_2,
        LV_MOD1       TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES2     TYPE DEC5_2,

        LV_FM_NAME    TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_TOTCNT     TYPE INT4,
        LV_FRIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TOIDX      TYPE INT4,
        LV_TIMES      TYPE DEC5_2,
        LV_MOD        TYPE INT4.

  SORT PT_ZSDS0021 BY VBELN POSNR.
* Data 2개 Internal Table로 이동
  CLEAR LV_LOOPCNT.
  LOOP AT PT_ZSDS0020 INTO LS_ZSDS0020.
    ADD  1  TO  LV_LOOPCNT.
    LOOP AT PT_ZSDS0021 INTO LS_ZSDS0021 WHERE VBELN = LS_ZSDS0020-VBELN
    .
      IF LV_LOOPCNT = 1.
        LS_ZSDS0021_1 = LS_ZSDS0021.
        APPEND LS_ZSDS0021_1 TO LT_ZSDS0021_1.
      ELSE.
        LS_ZSDS0021_2 = LS_ZSDS0021.
        APPEND LS_ZSDS0021_2 TO LT_ZSDS0021_2.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
* 스마트폼 함수명 조회'
  PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_C'
                                CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.

* 스마트폼 호출 1페이지에 6행
  LV_TOTCNT = LINES( LT_ZSDS0021_1 ).
  LV_TOTCNT1 = LINES( LT_ZSDS0021_2 ).
* 6건씩 출력.
  LV_TIMES  = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT / 6 ).
  LV_TIMES1 = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT1 / 6 ).
* 나머지
  LV_MOD   = LV_TOTCNT MOD 6.
  LV_MOD1  = LV_TOTCNT1 MOD 6.
* 나머지가 있으면 나머지 전송하기 위해 1을 더한다.
  IF LV_MOD GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES.
  ENDIF.
  IF LV_MOD1 GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES1.
  ENDIF.
  IF LV_TIMES >= LV_TIMES1.
    LV_TIMES2 = LV_TIMES.
  ELSE.
    LV_TIMES2 = LV_TIMES1.
  ENDIF.
* From Index
  LV_FRIDX = 1.
  LV_FRIDX1 = 1.
* 6건씩 출력.
  DO LV_TIMES2 TIMES.
    CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0021.
*   미리보기 설정
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
*   첫페이지
*    IF P_TABIX EQ 1 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
    IF P_TABIX < 3 AND SY-INDEX EQ 1.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   마지막페이지
    IF P_TABIX EQ P_ROWCNT AND SY-INDEX EQ LV_TIMES2.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   To Index
    ADD 6 TO LV_TOIDX.
    IF LV_TOIDX GT LV_TOTCNT.
      LV_TOIDX = LV_TOTCNT.
    ENDIF.
    ADD 6 TO LV_TOIDX1.
    IF LV_TOIDX1 GT LV_TOTCNT1.
      LV_TOIDX1 = LV_TOTCNT1.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LINES OF LT_ZSDS0021_1 FROM LV_FRIDX TO LV_TOIDX TO
    LT_ZSDS0021.
    ADD 6 TO LV_FRIDX.
    IF  LV_TOIDX1 NE 0.
      APPEND LINES OF LT_ZSDS0021_2 FROM LV_FRIDX1 TO LV_TOIDX1 TO
      LT_ZSDS0021.
      ADD 6 TO LV_FRIDX1.
    ENDIF.

    DATA : L_USER_SETTINGS TYPE C.
    CLEAR: L_USER_SETTINGS.
    PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS-TDDEST         = 'CHIN'.

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
        USER_SETTINGS      = L_USER_SETTINGS
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDDO.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_0100_C
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ZEX401_ZEXDCL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       통관상태
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ZEX401_ZEXDCL USING P_VBELN
                        CHANGING P_ZEXDCL.

  CLEAR: P_ZEXDCL.
  CHECK P_VBELN IS NOT INITIAL.

  SELECT SINGLE ZEXDCL
    INTO P_ZEXDCL
    FROM ZEX401
   WHERE AUTYP EQ 'J'
     AND AUBEL EQ P_VBELN.

ENDFORM. " GET_ZEX401_ZEXDCL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_VBKD_KDKG1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       장기재고그룹
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_VBKD_KDKG1 USING P_VBELN
                          P_POSNR
                 CHANGING P_KDKG1.

  CLEAR: P_KDKG1.

  SELECT SINGLE KDKG1
    INTO P_KDKG1
    FROM VBKD
   WHERE VBELN EQ P_VBELN
     AND POSNR EQ P_POSNR.

ENDFORM. " GET_VBKD_KDKG1

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_TVKGGT_VTEXT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       장기재고그룹명
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_TVKGGT_VTEXT USING P_KDKG1
                   CHANGING P_KDTX1.

  CLEAR: P_KDTX1.
  CHECK P_KDKG1 IS NOT INITIAL.

  SELECT SINGLE VTEXT
    INTO P_KDTX1
    FROM TVKGGT
   WHERE SPRAS EQ SY-LANGU
     AND KDKGR EQ P_KDKG1.

ENDFORM. " GET_TVKGGT_VTEXT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_JOB_ORDER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       작업지시서 출력
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_JOB_ORDER .

  DATA: LT_ROWS               TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
        LT_HEAD_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_DATA_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_ZSDTY0022          TYPE ZSDTY0022,
        LS_ROWS               LIKE LINE OF LT_ROWS,
        LS_HEAD_0100          LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_DATA_0100          LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_ZSDTY0022          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0022,
        LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP,
        LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP,
        LV_FM_NAME            TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_VKBTX              TYPE ZSDS0022-VKBTX,
*/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822
*        lv_name1              TYPE zsds0022-name1,
        L_ZPONUM              TYPE ZSDS0022-ZPONUM,
        L_VKBTX               TYPE ZSDS0022-VKBTX,
        L_NAME1               TYPE ZSDS0022-NAME1,
*/E
        LV_SLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_TLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_TOTAL              TYPE LIPS-LFIMG.

  CALL METHOD GV_GRID_0100->GET_SELECTED_ROWS
    IMPORTING
      ET_INDEX_ROWS = LT_ROWS.
* 선택한 행의 데이터로 테이블생성
  LOOP AT LT_ROWS INTO LS_ROWS WHERE ROWTYPE EQ SPACE.
    CLEAR: LS_DATA_0100.
    READ TABLE GT_DATA_0100 INDEX LS_ROWS-INDEX
                             INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_HEAD_0100.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF LT_HEAD_0100 IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE S006.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.
* 납품문서 기준으로 헤더생성
  SORT LT_HEAD_0100 BY VBELN INCO2.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES
    FROM LT_HEAD_0100 COMPARING VBELN.
  LOOP AT LT_HEAD_0100 INTO LS_HEAD_0100.
    LOOP AT GT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100
                        WHERE VBELN EQ LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

*/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822
      CASE LS_DATA_0100-VKGRP.
        WHEN '300'.
*     무역부-고객사 P/O 사용
        WHEN OTHERS.
*     P/O 번호 출력
          LS_DATA_0100-BSTKD = LS_DATA_0100-ZPONUM.
      ENDCASE.
*/E
      APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_DATA_0100.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.

*/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822
*  SORT lt_data_0100 BY kbtx name1 wadat.
  SORT LT_DATA_0100 BY VKBTX BSTKD WADAT.
  READ TABLE LT_DATA_0100 INDEX 1 INTO LS_DATA_0100.

*  lv_name1 = ls_data_0100-name1.
  L_ZPONUM = LS_DATA_0100-BSTKD. "PO
  L_VKBTX  = LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX. "사무소
  L_NAME1  = LS_DATA_0100-NAME1. "납품처
*/E
  LV_VKBTX = LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX.
*/삼호중공업의 경우 거래명세서에  삼호사무소로 표기 -swlee-20210729
  IF LV_VKBTX EQ '삼호중공업'.
    LV_VKBTX = '삼호사무소'.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT LT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
**   소계, 합계
**/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822
**   거래처가 틀려질 경우 소계
**    IF lv_name1 NE ls_data_0100-name1.
*    IF L_ZPONUM NE LS_DATA_0100-BSTKD.
**/E
*      CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.
*      LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**소계**'.
*      WRITE LV_SLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*      LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*      APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.
*      CLEAR: LV_SLFIMG.
**      lv_name1 = ls_data_0100-name1.
*      L_ZPONUM = LS_DATA_0100-BSTKD.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    IF L_VKBTX  NE LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX.
*      CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.
*      LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**소계**'.
*      WRITE LV_SLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*      LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*      APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.
*      CLEAR: LV_SLFIMG.
*      L_VKBTX = LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX.
*    ENDIF.

    IF L_NAME1  NE LS_DATA_0100-NAME1.
      CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.
      LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**소계**'.
      WRITE LV_SLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
      LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
      APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.
      CLEAR: LV_SLFIMG.
      L_NAME1  = LS_DATA_0100-NAME1.
    ENDIF.


*   소계
    ADD LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG TO LV_SLFIMG.
*   총계
    ADD LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG TO LV_TOTAL.
*   수주부서가 틀려질 경우 합계
    IF LV_VKBTX NE LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX.
      CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.
      LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**합계**'.
*      ls_zsdty0022-lfimg = lv_tlfimg.
      WRITE LV_TLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
      LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
      APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.

      CLEAR: LV_TLFIMG.
      LV_VKBTX = LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX.
    ENDIF.
*   합계
    ADD LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG TO LV_TLFIMG.

    CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_DATA_0100 TO LS_ZSDTY0022.
*   일자
    WRITE LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT TO LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT
      USING EDIT MASK '____-__-__'.
*   수량
    WRITE LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT
    LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*   플랜트명
    PERFORM GET_T001W_NAME1 USING LS_DATA_0100-WERKS
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0022-WERTX.
*   팔레트별 중량명
    PERFORM GET_TVM5T_BEZEI USING LS_DATA_0100-MVGR5
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0022-MVTX5.
*   판매유형 명칭
    PERFORM GET_TVAKT_BEZEI USING LS_DATA_0100-AUART
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0022-AUATX.
**   P/O 번호
*    CASE ls_data_0100-vkgrp.
*      WHEN '300'.
**     무역부-고객사 P/O 사용
*      WHEN OTHERS.
**     P/O 번호 출력
*        ls_zsdty0022-bstkd = ls_zsdty0022-zponum.
*    ENDCASE.

*   납품문서번호(2013/04/23)
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT'
      EXPORTING
        INPUT  = LS_ZSDTY0022-VBELN
      IMPORTING
        OUTPUT = LS_ZSDTY0022-VBELN.

    "QA 테스트 자재 추가_천준필_202012116
    LS_ZSDTY0022-QAFLG = LS_DATA_0100-ZQATEST.

    APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.

  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.
  LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**소계**'.
  WRITE LV_SLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*  ls_zsdty0022-lfimg = lv_slfimg.
  LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
  APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.

  LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**합계**'.
  WRITE LV_TLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*  ls_zsdty0022-lfimg = lv_tlfimg.
  LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
  APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.

  LS_ZSDTY0022-WADAT = '**총계**'.
  WRITE LV_TOTAL TO LS_ZSDTY0022-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*  ls_zsdty0022-lfimg = lv_total.
  LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
  APPEND LS_ZSDTY0022 TO LT_ZSDTY0022.

  call function 'ZSD_DOWNLOAD_DOCUMENT'
    EXPORTING
      PV_APP_NAME        = '일일작업지시서'
      PV_OBJECT_ID       = 'ZSDR0020'
      PV_MACRO_NAME      = 'Macro1'
      PV_STRUCTURE_NAME1 = 'DATA1'
    TABLES
      PT_DATA1           = LT_ZSDTY0022.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_JOB_ORDER

*작업지시서(1A,1C,1G,1H 플랜트변경)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_JOB_ORDER1 .

  DATA: LT_ROWS               TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
        LT_HEAD_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_DATA_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_ZSDTY0022          TYPE ZSDTY0022,
        LS_ROWS               LIKE LINE OF LT_ROWS,

        LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP,

*/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822
*        lv_name1              TYPE zsds0022-name1,

        L_VKBTX               TYPE ZSDS0022-VKBTX,
        L_NAME1               TYPE ZSDS0022-NAME1,
*/E

        LV_TLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,

    IMPORTING
      ET_INDEX_ROWS = LT_ROWS.
* 선택한 행의 데이터로 테이블생성
  LOOP AT LT_ROWS INTO LS_ROWS WHERE ROWTYPE EQ SPACE.
    CLEAR: LS_DATA_0100.
    READ TABLE GT_DATA_0100 INDEX LS_ROWS-INDEX
                             INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_HEAD_0100.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF LT_HEAD_0100 IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE S006.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.
* 납품문서 기준으로 헤더생성

                        WHERE VBELN EQ LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

*/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822

        WHEN '300'.
*     무역부-고객사 P/O 사용
        WHEN OTHERS.
*     P/O 번호 출력
          LS_DATA_0100-BSTKD = LS_DATA_0100-ZPONUM.
      ENDCASE.
*/E

*/S
      CASE LS_DATA_0100-MATKL .
        WHEN '1A'.
          IF LS_DATA_0100-WERKS = '1010'.
            LS_DATA_0100-WERKS ='1020'.
          ENDIF.

        WHEN '1C'.
          IF LS_DATA_0100-WERKS = '1010'.
            LS_DATA_0100-WERKS ='1020'.
          ENDIF.

        WHEN '1G'.
          IF LS_DATA_0100-WERKS = '1010'.
            LS_DATA_0100-WERKS ='1020'.
          ENDIF.

        WHEN '1H'.
          IF LS_DATA_0100-WERKS = '1010'.
            LS_DATA_0100-WERKS ='1020'.
          ENDIF.
      ENDCASE.
*/E
      APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_DATA_0100.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.

*/S_작업지시서 SORT 기준 변경(거래처>PO)_천준필_20180822

*/E

        CLEAR: LV_SLFIMG.
        L_ZPONUM = LS_DATA_0100-BSTKD.
      ENDIF.

    ELSE.
      IF  LS_DATA_0100-VSTEL = '1011'  OR LS_DATA_0100-VSTEL = '1021'. "포항공장(수출) , 포항2공장(수출)

        IF L_NAME1  NE LS_DATA_0100-NAME1. "납품처
          CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0022.

          CLEAR: LV_SLFIMG.

      LS_ZSDTY0022-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.

                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0022-WERTX.

                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0022-MVTX5.
*   판매유형 명칭
    PERFORM GET_TVAKT_BEZEI USING LS_DATA_0100-AUART
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0022-AUATX.
**   P/O 번호
*    CASE ls_data_0100-vkgrp.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_JOB_ORDER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       DOOR 작업지시서 출력
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_JOB2_ORDER .

  DATA: LT_ROWS               TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
        LT_HEAD_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_DATA_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_ZSDTY0023          TYPE ZSDTY0023,
        LT_ZSDTY0024          TYPE ZSDTY0023,
        LS_ROWS               LIKE LINE OF LT_ROWS,
        LS_HEAD_0100          LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_DATA_0100          LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_ZSDTY0023          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0023,
        LS_ZSDTY0024          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0023,
        LS_ZSDTY0025          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0023,
        LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP,
        LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP,
        LV_FM_NAME            TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_VKBTX              TYPE ZSDS0023-VKBTX,
        LV_WADAT              TYPE ZSDS0023-WADAT,
        LV_NAME2              TYPE ZSDS0023-NAME2,
        LV_VGBEL              TYPE ZSDS0023-VGBEL,
        LV_SLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_TLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_ZPAL               TYPE P DECIMALS 0,
        LV_TOTAL              TYPE LIPS-LFIMG.

  CALL METHOD GV_GRID_0100->GET_SELECTED_ROWS
    IMPORTING
      ET_INDEX_ROWS = LT_ROWS.
* 선택한 행의 데이터로 테이블생성
  LOOP AT LT_ROWS INTO LS_ROWS WHERE ROWTYPE EQ SPACE.
    CLEAR: LS_DATA_0100.
    READ TABLE GT_DATA_0100 INDEX LS_ROWS-INDEX
                             INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_HEAD_0100.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF LT_HEAD_0100 IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE S006.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.
* 납품문서 기준으로 헤더생성
  SORT LT_HEAD_0100 BY VBELN.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES
    FROM LT_HEAD_0100 COMPARING VBELN.
  LOOP AT LT_HEAD_0100 INTO LS_HEAD_0100.
    LOOP AT GT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100
                        WHERE VBELN EQ LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.
      APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_DATA_0100.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT LT_DATA_0100 BY VKBTX VGBEL WADAT NAME2.
  READ TABLE LT_DATA_0100 INDEX 1 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
  LV_NAME2 = LS_DATA_0100-NAME2.
  LV_VKBTX = LS_DATA_0100-VKBTX.
  LV_WADAT = LS_DATA_0100-WADAT.
  LV_VGBEL  = LS_DATA_0100-VGBEL.

  LOOP AT LT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
*   소계, 합계
*   거래처가 틀려질 경우 소계
    IF LV_VGBEL  NE LS_DATA_0100-VGBEL .
*      CLEAR: ls_zsdty0023.
*      ls_zsdty0023-wadat = '**소계**'.
      WRITE LV_SLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0023-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
      LS_ZSDTY0023-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
      APPEND LS_ZSDTY0023 TO LT_ZSDTY0023.
      CLEAR: LV_SLFIMG.
      LV_VGBEL  = LS_DATA_0100-VGBEL .
    ENDIF.
*   소계
    ADD LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG TO LV_SLFIMG.
*   파레트갯수 합계
    ADD LS_DATA_0100-ZPAL  TO LV_ZPAL.


    CLEAR: LS_ZSDTY0023.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_DATA_0100 TO LS_ZSDTY0023.
*   일자
    WRITE LS_ZSDTY0023-WADAT TO LS_ZSDTY0023-WADAT
      USING EDIT MASK '____-__-__'.
*   수량
    WRITE LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0023-LFIMG UNIT
    LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*   도착지
    PERFORM GET_T001W_NAME3 USING LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0023-INCO2.
*   판매유형 명칭 get_t001w_name1
    PERFORM GET_TVAKT_BEZEI  USING LS_DATA_0100-AUART
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0023-AUATX.
*   p/o 번호
    PERFORM GET_VBKD_BSTKD  USING LS_DATA_0100-VGBEL
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDTY0023-ZPONUM.
**   P/O 번호
*    CASE ls_data_0100-vkgrp.
*      WHEN '300'.
**     무역부-고객사 P/O 사용
*      WHEN OTHERS.
**     P/O 번호 출력
*        ls_zsdty0023-bstkd = ls_zsdty0023-zponum.
*    ENDCASE.
*   납품문서번호(2013/04/23)
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT'
      EXPORTING
        INPUT  = LS_ZSDTY0023-VGBEL
      IMPORTING
        OUTPUT = LS_ZSDTY0023-VGBEL.
*    APPEND ls_zsdty0023 TO lt_zsdty0023.
  ENDLOOP.

*  CLEAR: ls_zsdty0023.
*  ls_zsdty0023-wadat = '**소계**'.
  WRITE LV_SLFIMG TO LS_ZSDTY0023-LFIMG UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
  WRITE LV_ZPAL   TO LS_ZSDTY0023-ZPAL  UNIT LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
*  ls_zsdty0022-lfimg = lv_slfimg.
  LS_ZSDTY0023-MEINS = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
  APPEND LS_ZSDTY0023 TO LT_ZSDTY0023.

  call function 'ZSD_DOWNLOAD_DOCUMENT'
    EXPORTING
      PV_APP_NAME        = 'Door 작업지시서'
      PV_OBJECT_ID       = 'ZSDR0023'
      PV_MACRO_NAME      = 'Macro23'
      PV_STRUCTURE_NAME6 = 'DATA6'
    TABLES
      PT_DATA6           = LT_ZSDTY0023.

** 스마트폼 옵션
*  PERFORM set_output_options     CHANGING ls_output_options.
*  PERFORM set_control_parameters CHANGING ls_control_parameters.
*
** 스마트폼 함수명 조회
*  PERFORM ssf_function_module_name USING 'ZSDSF0020_5'
*                                CHANGING lv_fm_name.
*
*  CALL FUNCTION lv_fm_name
*    EXPORTING
*      control_parameters = ls_control_parameters
*      output_options     = ls_output_options
*    TABLES
*      t_item             = lt_zsdty0022
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      formatting_error   = 1
*      internal_error     = 2
*      send_error         = 3
*      user_canceled      = 4
*      OTHERS             = 5.
*
*  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
*           WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
*  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_JOB_ORDER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_JOB5_ORDER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       거래명세표엑셀
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM PRINT_JOB5_ORDER USING P_TYPE .

  DATA: LT_ROWS               TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
        LT_HEAD_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_DATA_0100          LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_ZSDTY0023          TYPE ZSDTY0023,
        LT_ZSDTY0024          TYPE ZSDTY0023,
        LS_ROWS               LIKE LINE OF LT_ROWS,
        LS_HEAD_0100          LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_DATA_0100          LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_ZSDTY0023          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0023,
        LS_ZSDTY0024          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0023,
        LS_ZSDTY0025          LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDTY0023,
        LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP,
        LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP,
        LV_FM_NAME            TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
        LV_VKBTX              TYPE ZSDS0023-VKBTX,
        LV_WADAT              TYPE ZSDS0023-WADAT,
        LV_NAME2              TYPE ZSDS0023-NAME2,
        LV_VGBEL              TYPE ZSDS0023-VGBEL,
        LV_SLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_TLFIMG             TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_ZPAL               TYPE P DECIMALS 0,
        LV_TOTAL              TYPE LIPS-LFIMG.

  DATA: LT_ZSDS0020           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ZSDS0020,
        LT_ZSDS0021           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ZSDS0021,
*        LT_ZSDS0025           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ZSDS0025,
        LT_LINE               TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF TLINE,
        LS_ZSDS0020           LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDS0020,
        LS_ZSDS0021           LIKE LINE OF LT_ZSDS0021,
        LS_LINE               LIKE LINE OF LT_LINE,
        LV_NAME               TYPE THEAD-TDNAME,
        LV_SEQNR              TYPE SEQNR2,
        LV_ADRNR              TYPE ADRC-ADDRNUMBER,
        LV_ADRDA              TYPE VBPA-ADRDA,
        LV_TABIX              TYPE INT4,
        LV_HEADCNT            TYPE INT4,
        LV_ROWCNT             TYPE INT4,
        LV_ANSWER             TYPE CHAR01,
        LV_VBELN_TMP        TYPE C LENGTH 10,
        LV_VBELN_TMP_OLD        TYPE C LENGTH 10. "이전 납품문서와 비교을 위함



  CALL METHOD GV_GRID_0100->GET_SELECTED_ROWS
    IMPORTING
      ET_INDEX_ROWS = LT_ROWS.
* 선택한 행의 데이터로 테이블생성
  LOOP AT LT_ROWS INTO LS_ROWS WHERE ROWTYPE EQ SPACE.
    CLEAR: LS_DATA_0100.
    READ TABLE GT_DATA_0100 INDEX LS_ROWS-INDEX
                             INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_HEAD_0100.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF LT_HEAD_0100 IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE S006.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* 단가/금액 출력
  IF P_TYPE = '5' .
    CLEAR GV_PRINT.
    CLEAR: RG_01, RG_02.
    CALL SCREEN 0700 STARTING AT 20 1
                     ENDING   AT 45 5.
*    CALL SCREEN 0700 .
    IF RG_02 = 'X'.
      GV_PRINT = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* 납품문서 기준으로 헤더생성
  SORT LT_HEAD_0100 BY VBELN.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES
    FROM LT_HEAD_0100 COMPARING VBELN.


  LV_ROWCNT = LINES( LT_HEAD_0100 ).

  CLEAR LV_HEADCNT.

  LOOP AT LT_HEAD_0100 INTO LS_HEAD_0100.
    IF P_TYPE = '1'.
      CLEAR: LV_SEQNR.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0020, LT_ZSDS0021, LT_LINE, LS_LINE, LS_ZSDS0020,
      LV_NAME, LV_SEQNR.
    ENDIF.

    ADD 1 TO LV_HEADCNT.
    LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_HEAD_0100 TO LS_ZSDS0020.

    PERFORM GET_TVST_ADRNR USING LS_HEAD_0100-VSTEL
                        CHANGING LV_ADRNR.
    PERFORM GET_ADRC USING LV_ADRNR
                  CHANGING LS_ZSDS0020-TEL_NUMBER1
                           LS_ZSDS0020-CITY1.

    CASE LS_HEAD_0100-VKGRP.
      WHEN '300'.
        IF LS_HEAD_0100-INCO1 EQ 'FOB'.
          CLEAR: LS_ZSDS0020-NAME1.
        ELSE.
          LS_ZSDS0020-NAME1 = LS_HEAD_0100-INCO2.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN OTHERS.
        PERFORM GET_VBPA_ADRDA USING LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN
                                     'WE'
                            CHANGING LV_ADRDA.



        PERFORM GET_VBPA_ADRNR USING LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN
                            CHANGING LV_ADRNR.
        PERFORM GET_ADRC_TEL_NUMBER USING LV_ADRNR
                                 CHANGING LS_ZSDS0020-TEL_NUMBER2
                                          LS_ZSDS0020-NAME1.

    ENDCASE.

    PERFORM GET_HEADER_INFO USING P_TYPE
                                  LS_HEAD_0100
                         CHANGING LS_ZSDS0020.


    IF LS_HEAD_0100-COMMT EQ ICON_LED_GREEN.
      LV_NAME = LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

      CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
        EXPORTING
          CLIENT   = SY-MANDT
          ID       = '0001'
          LANGUAGE = SY-LANGU
          NAME     = LV_NAME
          OBJECT   = 'VBBK'
        TABLES
          LINES    = LT_LINE.

      READ TABLE LT_LINE INDEX 1 INTO LS_LINE.
      LS_ZSDS0020-TDLINE1 = LS_LINE-TDLINE.

      CLEAR: LS_LINE.
      READ TABLE LT_LINE INDEX 2 INTO LS_LINE.
      LS_ZSDS0020-TDLINE2 = LS_LINE-TDLINE.

      CLEAR: LS_LINE.
      READ TABLE LT_LINE INDEX 3 INTO LS_LINE.
      LS_ZSDS0020-TDLINE3 = LS_LINE-TDLINE.
    ENDIF.




    LOOP AT GT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100
                        WHERE VBELN EQ LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

      IF RG_01 = 'X' .
        LS_DATA_0100-NETPR = '0'.
        LS_DATA_0100-SALES = '0'.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR: LS_ZSDS0021.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_DATA_0100 TO LS_ZSDS0021.


      PERFORM GET_MVGR3_TEXT1 USING  LS_ZSDS0021-MVGR3
                           CHANGING  LS_ZSDS0021-BEZEI.

      WRITE LS_DATA_0100-POSNR TO LS_ZSDS0021-POSNR NO-ZERO.

      ADD 1 TO LV_SEQNR.
      LS_ZSDS0021-SEQNR = LV_SEQNR.

      CASE LS_DATA_0100-VKGRP.
        WHEN '300'.

        WHEN OTHERS.

          LS_ZSDS0021-BSTKD = LS_ZSDS0021-ZPONUM.
      ENDCASE.

      ADD LS_ZSDS0021-LFIMG TO LS_ZSDS0020-TOTAL.
      LS_ZSDS0020-MEINS = LS_ZSDS0021-MEINS.


      IF P_TYPE = '2' AND GV_PRINT = 'X'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_MATN1_OUTPUT'
          EXPORTING
            INPUT  = LS_HEAD_0100-MATNR
          IMPORTING
            OUTPUT = LS_HEAD_0100-MATNR.
        CASE LS_HEAD_0100-MATNR+1(1).
          WHEN 'Z'.
            CONCATENATE LS_ZSDS0021-ZMAKTX '(장기재고품)'
            INTO LS_ZSDS0021-ZMAKTX SEPARATED BY SPACE.
        ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND LS_ZSDS0021 TO LT_ZSDS0021.

    ENDLOOP.


    APPEND LS_ZSDS0020 TO LT_ZSDS0020.


  ENDLOOP.

********************ZSDS0020 HEADER , ZSDS0021 ITEM, LS_FLAT.
  DATA: BEGIN OF LS_FLAT.
          include structure ZSDS0020. " 헤더 필드들
  DATA:   POSNR  TYPE ZSDS0021-POSNR, " 아이템 필드들 추가

          ZMAKTX TYPE ZSDS0021-ZMAKTX,
          GROES  TYPE ZSDS0021-GROES,
          LFIMG  TYPE ZSDS0021-LFIMG,
*        MEINS  TYPE ZSDS0021-MEINS,
          NETPR  TYPE ZSDS0021-NETPR,
          SALES  TYPE ZSDS0021-SALES,
*        WAERK  TYPE ZSDS0021-WAERK,
          VGBEL  TYPE ZSDS0021-VGBEL,
          BEZEI  TYPE ZSDS0021-BEZEI,
          BSTKD_E  TYPE ZSDS0021-BSTKD_E,

          " ... 필요한 아이템 필드들 추가
        END OF LS_FLAT.

  DATA: LT_FLAT LIKE STANDARD TABLE OF LS_FLAT.

  LOOP AT LT_ZSDS0021 INTO LS_ZSDS0021.
    " 1. 아이템 데이터 먼저 복사
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_ZSDS0021 TO LS_FLAT.

    " 2. 해당 아이템의 헤더 정보를 읽어와서 합침
    READ TABLE LT_ZSDS0020 INTO LS_ZSDS0020
         WITH KEY VBELN = LS_ZSDS0021-VBELN. " VBELN은 예시 키값입니다
    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING LS_ZSDS0020 TO LS_FLAT.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LS_FLAT TO LT_FLAT.
    CLEAR LS_FLAT.
  ENDLOOP.


  call function 'ZSD_DOWNLOAD_DOCUMENT'
    EXPORTING
      PV_APP_NAME        = '거래명세표엑셀'
      PV_OBJECT_ID       = 'ZSDR0020DEAL'
      PV_MACRO_NAME      = 'Macro23'
      PV_STRUCTURE_NAME6 = 'DATA6'
    TABLES
*     PT_DATA6           = LT_ZSDTY0023.
      PT_DATA6           = LT_FLAT.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_JOB_ORDER

ENDFORM. "get_t001w_name1

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ADRC_NAME1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       주소
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ADRC_NAME1 USING P_VBELN
                              P_PARVW
                     CHANGING P_NAME1.

  DATA: LV_ADRNR TYPE VBPA-ADRNR.

  CLEAR: P_NAME1.
  CHECK P_VBELN IS NOT INITIAL.

  SELECT SINGLE ADRNR
    INTO LV_ADRNR
    FROM VBPA
   WHERE VBELN EQ P_VBELN
     AND PARVW EQ P_PARVW.

  CHECK SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
  SELECT SINGLE NAME1
    INTO P_NAME1
    FROM ADRC
   WHERE ADDRNUMBER EQ LV_ADRNR.

ENDFORM. " GET_ADRC_NAME1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ADRC_NAME2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ADRC_NAME2 USING P_VBELN
                              P_PARVW
                     CHANGING P_NAME1 P_NAME2.

  DATA: LV_ADRNR TYPE VBPA-ADRNR.

  CLEAR: P_NAME1, P_NAME2.
  CHECK P_VBELN IS NOT INITIAL.

  SELECT SINGLE ADRNR
    INTO LV_ADRNR
    FROM VBPA
   WHERE VBELN EQ P_VBELN
     AND PARVW EQ P_PARVW.

  CHECK SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
  SELECT SINGLE NAME1 NAME2
    INTO (P_NAME1, P_NAME2)
    FROM ADRC
   WHERE ADDRNUMBER EQ LV_ADRNR.

ENDFORM. " GET_ADRC_NAME2

     AND PARVW EQ P_PARVW.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REFRESH_DATA_0100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM REFRESH_DATA_0100 .
  DATA: LT_DATA_0100   LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_DATA_0100_1 LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LR_STATS       TYPE RANGE OF ICON-ID,
        LR_STATZ       TYPE RANGE OF ICON-ID,
        LS_DATA_0100   LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_STATS       LIKE LINE OF LR_STATS,
        LS_STATZ       LIKE LINE OF LR_STATZ,
        LV_ROWID       TYPE INT4,
        LV_MENGE       TYPE LIPS-LFIMG,
        LV_ROWCOUNT    TYPE CHAR10.

  LOOP AT GT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    CLEAR LIPS.
*    SELECT SINGLE *
*      FROM lips
*     WHERE vgbel = ls_data_0100-vgbel
*       AND vgpos = ls_data_0100-vgpos.
    SELECT SINGLE *
      FROM LIPS
     WHERE VBELN = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
       AND POSNR = LS_DATA_0100-POSNR.
    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
      LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG = LIPS-LFIMG.
      LS_DATA_0100-XCHPF = LIPS-XCHPF.
      LS_DATA_0100-XCHAR = LIPS-XCHAR.
      LS_DATA_0100-LGORT = LIPS-LGORT.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR : LS_DATA_0100-RFIMG, LS_DATA_0100-CFIMG, LS_DATA_0100-STATS,
            LS_DATA_0100-SALES, LS_DATA_0100-STATZ,
            LS_DATA_0100-RJAHR, LS_DATA_0100-RBLNR, LS_DATA_0100-RUDAT,
            LS_DATA_0100-MJAHR, LS_DATA_0100-MBLNR, LS_DATA_0100-BUDAT,
            LS_DATA_0100-GIMSG, LS_DATA_0100-MSGTB.
*   [적송] 출하수량
    IF LS_DATA_0100-LGORT EQ '9000' OR
       LS_DATA_0100-LGORT EQ 'CY00'.
      LS_DATA_0100-RFIMG = LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG.
    ENDIF.
*   [확정] 출하수량
    PERFORM GET_VBUK_WBSTK USING LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
                        CHANGING LS_DATA_0100-WBSTK.
    IF LS_DATA_0100-WBSTK EQ 'C'.
      LS_DATA_0100-CFIMG = LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG.
    ENDIF.
*   상태
    IF LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG EQ LS_DATA_0100-RFIMG OR
       LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG EQ LS_DATA_0100-CFIMG.
*     출하 상태
      LS_DATA_0100-STATS = ICON_LED_GREEN.
    ENDIF.
    LV_MENGE = LS_DATA_0100-RFIMG + LS_DATA_0100-CFIMG.
    IF LV_MENGE EQ 0.
*     미출하 상태
      LS_DATA_0100-STATS = ICON_LED_YELLOW.
    ENDIF.

*   출하상태
    IF LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG EQ '0' AND
       LS_DATA_0100-XCHPF EQ 'X' AND
       LS_DATA_0100-XCHAR EQ 'X'.
      LS_DATA_0100-STATS = ICON_LED_GREEN.
    ENDIF.


*   통관상태
    PERFORM GET_ZEX401_ZEXDCL USING LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
                           CHANGING LS_DATA_0100-ZEXDCL.
    IF LS_DATA_0100-ZEXDCL IS NOT INITIAL.
      LS_DATA_0100-STATZ = ICON_LED_GREEN.
    ELSE.
      LS_DATA_0100-STATZ = ICON_LED_YELLOW.
    ENDIF.



*   납품유형  'NL' 판매처에 납품처 대입.
    IF LS_DATA_0100-LFART EQ 'NL'.
      LS_DATA_0100-KUNAG = LS_DATA_0100-KUNNR.
    ENDIF.

    LS_DATA_0100-SALES = ( LS_DATA_0100-NETPR / LS_DATA_0100-KPEIN ) *
    LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG.


*   [적송] 출하수량 존재시
    IF LS_DATA_0100-RFIMG NE SPACE.
*     [적송] 자재전표연도, [적송] 자재문서번호
      SELECT SINGLE MJAHR MBLNR
        INTO (LS_DATA_0100-RJAHR, LS_DATA_0100-RBLNR)
        FROM ZSDT0020
       WHERE VBELN  EQ LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
         AND CMBLNR EQ SPACE.
*     [적송] 출하일자
      PERFORM GET_MKPF_BUDAT USING LS_DATA_0100-RJAHR
                                   LS_DATA_0100-RBLNR
                          CHANGING LS_DATA_0100-RUDAT.
    ENDIF.
*   [확정] 출하수량 존재시
    IF LS_DATA_0100-CFIMG NE SPACE.
*     [확정] 자재전표연도, [확정] 자재문서번호
      PERFORM GET_VBFA USING LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
                             LS_DATA_0100-POSNR
                    CHANGING LS_DATA_0100-MJAHR
                             LS_DATA_0100-MBLNR.
*     [확정] 출하일자
      PERFORM GET_MKPF_BUDAT USING LS_DATA_0100-MJAHR
                                   LS_DATA_0100-MBLNR
                          CHANGING LS_DATA_0100-BUDAT.
    ENDIF.

*     2015/05/13 추가
    IF ( LS_DATA_0100-AUART = 'ZOR' AND  LS_DATA_0100-LGORT  = '9000 '
    AND  LS_DATA_0100-RBLNR  =  ' ' ) OR ( LS_DATA_0100-AUART = 'ZOR'
    AND  LS_DATA_0100-LGORT  = 'CY00 ' AND  LS_DATA_0100-RBLNR  =  ' ' )
    .
      LS_DATA_0100-STATS = ICON_LED_RED.
    ENDIF.

** 2026.04.27 NYG 출하처리상태
    IF GR_VBELN[] IS NOT INITIAL AND LS_DATA_0100-VBELN IN GR_VBELN.
      LS_DATA_0100-ZSTATS = GV_ZSTATS.
      CLEAR GS_TEXT.
      READ TABLE GT_TEXT INTO GS_TEXT WITH KEY DOMNAME    = 'ZDSTATS'
                                               DOMVALUE_L = LS_DATA_0100-ZSTATS.
      IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
        LS_DATA_0100-ZSTATS_TXT = GS_TEXT-DDTEXT.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
** 2026.04.27 NYG 출하처리상태

    MODIFY GT_DATA_0100 FROM LS_DATA_0100.


  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM. " REFRESH_DATA_0100

*&      Form  GET_HEADER_INFO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_HEADER_INFO USING P_TYPE
                               PS_HEAD_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100
                      CHANGING CS_ZSDS0020 LIKE ZSDS0020.
*>> FOR CHINA HEADER INFO
  IF P_TYPE = 'C'.
    CASE PS_HEAD_0100-VTWEG.
      WHEN '10'."판매처명을 출력
        IF PS_HEAD_0100-LFART EQ 'NL'.
          PERFORM GET_KUNNR_TEXT2 USING PS_HEAD_0100-KUNAG
                               CHANGING CS_ZSDS0020-NAME1
                                        CS_ZSDS0020-NAME2.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM GET_ADRC_NAME2 USING PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN 'AG'
                              CHANGING CS_ZSDS0020-NAME1
                                       CS_ZSDS0020-NAME2.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN '20'.
        PERFORM GET_ADRC_NAME2 USING PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN 'WE'
                            CHANGING CS_ZSDS0020-NAME1
                                     CS_ZSDS0020-NAME2.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " GET_HEADER_INFO

FORM AUTHORITY_CHECK_VKORG_SO USING P_GB.

* VKORG CODE
  DATA: LT_TVKO LIKE TVKO OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
  SELECT * FROM TVKO INTO TABLE LT_TVKO WHERE VKORG IN SO_VKORG.

  LOOP AT LT_TVKO.
    PERFORM AUTHORITY_CHECK_VKORG_PA(ZSDR0010) USING LT_TVKO-VKORG P_GB.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM. " AUTHORITY_CHECK_VKORG_SO

*&      Form  SET_NAME_TABLE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM SET_NAME_TABLE TABLES PT_DATA LIKE GT_DATA_0100.

  CHECK PT_DATA[] IS NOT INITIAL.

  CLEAR: GT_KNA1,GT_KNA1[].

  SELECT * INTO TABLE GT_KNA1
    FROM KNA1
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN PT_DATA
   WHERE KUNNR = PT_DATA-KUNNR.

  SELECT * APPENDING TABLE GT_KNA1
    FROM KNA1
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN PT_DATA
   WHERE KUNNR = PT_DATA-KUNAG.
*
*  SELECT * APPENDING TABLE gt_kna1
*    FROM kna1
*     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN pt_data
*   WHERE kunnr = pt_data-punnr.
*
*  SELECT * APPENDING TABLE gt_kna1
*    FROM kna1
*     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN pt_data
*   WHERE kunnr = pt_data-yunnr1.
*
*  SELECT * APPENDING TABLE gt_kna1
*    FROM kna1
*     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN pt_data
*   WHERE kunnr = pt_data-yunnr2.

  SORT GT_KNA1 BY KUNNR.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM GT_KNA1.
ENDFORM. " SET_NAME_TABLE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_SUB_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM SELECT_SUB_DATA TABLES PT_DATA LIKE GT_DATA_0100.

  CHECK PT_DATA[] IS NOT INITIAL.

  CLEAR: GT_0516, GT_0516[].
  SELECT A~VBELN2 B~POSNR
         A~VBELN B~NETPR B~KPEIN B~KMEIN
    INTO TABLE GT_0516
    FROM ZSDT0516 AS A INNER JOIN
         VBAP     AS B
      ON A~VBELN2 = B~VBELN
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN PT_DATA
   WHERE A~VBELN2 = PT_DATA-VGBEL
     AND B~POSNR  = PT_DATA-POSNR.
  SORT GT_0516 BY VBELN2 POSNR.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM GT_0516.

ENDFORM. " SELECT_SUB_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ZSDT0516
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ZSDT0516 CHANGING PS_DATA LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.

  READ TABLE GT_0516 WITH KEY VBELN2 = PS_DATA-VBELN
                              POSNR  = PS_DATA-POSNR
                              BINARY SEARCH.
  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
    PS_DATA-KNETPR = GT_0516-NETPR.
    PS_DATA-KVBELN = GT_0516-VBELN.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " GET_ZSDT0516

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GI_LONG_TERM_STOCK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GI_LONG_TERM_STOCK USING T_DATA LIKE GT_DATA_0100
                                 P_TYPE.

  DATA: S_DATA  LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.
  DATA: T_LSTOCK TYPE TABLE OF ZMMT0350,
        S_LSTOCK TYPE ZMMT0350,
        V_KDKG1 TYPE VBKD-KDKG1,
        V_MENGE TYPE MENGE_D.

  LOOP AT T_DATA INTO S_DATA.
    CLEAR: V_KDKG1, V_MENGE.
    SELECT SINGLE KDKG1
    INTO (V_KDKG1)
    FROM VBKD
    WHERE VBELN = S_DATA-VGBEL
      AND POSNR = S_DATA-VGPOS.

    IF V_KDKG1 IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT *
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE T_LSTOCK
      FROM ZMMT0350
      WHERE MATNR = S_DATA-MATNR
        AND WERKS = S_DATA-WERKS
        AND LGORT = S_DATA-LGORT.

      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
        SORT T_LSTOCK BY GRMON.

        V_MENGE = S_DATA-LFIMG.
        LOOP AT T_LSTOCK INTO S_LSTOCK.
          IF V_MENGE <= 0.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.

          CASE P_TYPE.
            WHEN 'GI'.
              S_LSTOCK-MENGE = S_LSTOCK-MENGE - V_MENGE.

              IF S_LSTOCK-MENGE < 0.
                CLEAR V_MENGE.
                V_MENGE = S_LSTOCK-MENGE * -1.
                S_LSTOCK-MENGE = 0.
              ENDIF.

            WHEN 'GR'.
              S_LSTOCK-MENGE = S_LSTOCK-MENGE + V_MENGE.
              CLEAR V_MENGE.
          ENDCASE.

          MODIFY T_LSTOCK FROM S_LSTOCK TRANSPORTING MENGE.
        ENDLOOP.

        MODIFY ZMMT0350 FROM TABLE T_LSTOCK.
        COMMIT WORK.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM. " GI_LONG_TERM_STOCK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_PBAR_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LT_DATA_0100  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM SELECT_PBAR_DATA TABLES PT_DATA LIKE GT_DATA_0100.

  DATA : LV_TABIX TYPE SY-TABIX.

  CHECK PT_DATA[] IS NOT INITIAL.

  CLEAR: GS_PBAR, GT_PBAR.

  SELECT B~VBELN B~POSNR A~PBARCODE A~SBARCODE A~WERKS B~ZSTATS A~LGORTN
               B~MOVE_TYPE B~LFIMG A~VEHICLE_NO
       INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE GT_PBAR
      FROM ZSDT5031 AS A INNER JOIN ZSDT5030 AS B
          ON A~VBELN EQ B~VBELN
        AND A~POSNR EQ B~POSNR
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN PT_DATA
    WHERE B~MOVE_TYPE EQ 'GI' "공장 출하
        AND B~VBELN          EQ PT_DATA-VBELN
        AND B~POSNR         EQ PT_DATA-POSNR.

  SORT GT_PBAR BY VBELN POSNR.

  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE GT_TEXT
     FROM DD07T
   WHERE DOMNAME IN ('ZDMTYPE', 'ZDSTATS')
       AND DDLANGUAGE EQ '3'. "KO

  SORT GT_TEXT BY DOMNAME DOMVALUE_L.

  CLEAR LV_TABIX.
  LOOP AT GT_PBAR INTO GS_PBAR.
    LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.

    READ TABLE GT_TEXT INTO GS_TEXT WITH KEY DOMNAME = 'ZDMTYPE'
                                                                            DOMVALUE_L = GS_PBAR-MOVE_TYPE
                                                                            BINARY SEARCH.
    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      GS_PBAR-MOVE_TYPE_TXT = GS_TEXT-DDTEXT.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR GS_TEXT.
    READ TABLE GT_TEXT INTO GS_TEXT WITH KEY DOMNAME = 'ZDSTATS'
                                                                            DOMVALUE_L = GS_PBAR-ZSTATS
                                                                            BINARY SEARCH.
    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      GS_PBAR-ZSTATS_TXT = GS_TEXT-DDTEXT.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY GT_PBAR FROM GS_PBAR INDEX LV_TABIX.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM. " SELECT_PBAR_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MOVE_STOCK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM CALL_SCREEN_700 .

  DATA: LT_ROWS      TYPE LVC_T_ROW.

* 선택행 체크
  CALL METHOD GV_GRID_0100->GET_SELECTED_ROWS
    IMPORTING
      ET_INDEX_ROWS = LT_ROWS.

  IF LINES( LT_ROWS ) LE 0.
    MESSAGE S006.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CALL SCREEN '0700' STARTING AT 40 5.

ENDFORM. " MOVE_STOCK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GOODS_ISSUE_MOVE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GOODS_ISSUE_MOVE .


  DATA: LT_EMSG_0100 LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_ROWS      TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
        LT_HEAD_0100 LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_ITEM_0100 LIKE GT_DATA_0100,
        LT_RETURN    TYPE BAPIRET2_T,
        LS_ROWS      LIKE LINE OF LT_ROWS,
        LS_DATA_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_HEAD_0100 LIKE LINE OF LT_HEAD_0100,
        LS_RETURN    LIKE LINE OF LT_RETURN,
        LV_INDEX     TYPE SY-TABIX,
        LV_LGORT     TYPE LIPS-LGORT,
        LS_DATA_CHK LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.
*
  DATA : LV_TABIX TYPE SY-TABIX.

  DATA : LV_CHK TYPE C.

  DATA: V_PRCTR TYPE MARC-PRCTR.

  CALL METHOD GV_GRID_0100->GET_SELECTED_ROWS
    IMPORTING
      ET_INDEX_ROWS = LT_ROWS.

* 선택한 행의 데이터로 테이블생성
  LOOP AT LT_ROWS INTO LS_ROWS.
    CLEAR: LS_DATA_0100.
    READ TABLE GT_DATA_0100 INDEX LS_ROWS-INDEX
                             INTO LS_DATA_0100.

*/S_출하 시 포항 재고 사무소 출하 금지_천준필_20190703
    CASE SY-UNAME.
      WHEN 'HSD38' OR 'HSD48' OR 'HSD59' OR 'HSD81' OR 'HSD91' " HSD91 권한 추가_ 20230926
        OR 'HMM16' OR 'HTD01' OR 'HSD28'  OR 'HBC02' OR 'HIT01'. "포항 자재팀 자재 출하 권한 추가_20200521

      WHEN OTHERS.
        CASE LS_DATA_0100-VSTEL.
          WHEN '1010' OR '1011' OR '1020' OR '1021'.
            MESSAGE E048.
        ENDCASE.
    ENDCASE.
*/E

*/S_용접기사업부 분사 관련 수주 금지_천준필_20200228
    CLEAR: V_PRCTR.
    SELECT SINGLE PRCTR
    INTO  (V_PRCTR)
    FROM  MARC
    WHERE MATNR = LS_DATA_0100-MATNR
      AND WERKS = LS_DATA_0100-WERKS.
    IF SY-UNAME(3) <> 'HBC'.
      IF GV_DATE GE '20200301' AND V_PRCTR = '0000001520'.
        MESSAGE E057(ZSDM).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*/E

    IF LS_DATA_0100-CHECK IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE S000 WITH '팔레트 바코드가 존재하는 라인을 선택해주세요.'
      DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LS_DATA_0100 TO LT_HEAD_0100.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR : GS_0170, GT_0170, GS_5031, GT_5031, GS_5040, GT_5040.

  IF LT_HEAD_0100 IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE GT_0170
        FROM ZPPT0170
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN LT_HEAD_0100
      WHERE PBARCODE EQ LT_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
          AND STOCKSTATE EQ 'B'. "입고 상태

    SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE GT_5040
       FROM ZSDT5040
         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN LT_HEAD_0100
     WHERE PBARCODE EQ LT_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
         AND STATUS EQ '10'. "소분


    SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE GT_5031
        FROM ZSDT5031
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN LT_HEAD_0100
      WHERE VBELN          EQ LT_HEAD_0100-VBELN
          AND POSNR         EQ LT_HEAD_0100-POSNR
          AND PBARCODE   EQ LT_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
          AND SBARCODE   EQ LT_HEAD_0100-SBARCODE
          AND VEHICLE_NO EQ LT_HEAD_0100-VEHICLE_NO.
  ENDIF.

  SORT GT_0170 BY PBARCODE.
  SORT GT_5040 BY PBARCODE.
  SORT GT_5031 BY VBELN POSNR PBARCODE SBARCODE VEHICLE_NO.

  CLEAR GV_TABIX.
  IF GT_5040 IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT GT_5040 INTO GS_5040.
      GV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
      READ TABLE GT_0170 INTO GS_0170 WITH KEY PBARCODE = GS_5040-SBARCODE
                                                                           BINARY SEARCH.
      IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
        GS_5040-CHARG = GS_0170-CHARG.
        MODIFY GT_5040 FROM GS_5040 INDEX GV_TABIX.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR GV_TABIX.
  LOOP AT LT_HEAD_0100 INTO LS_HEAD_0100.
    GV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
    CLEAR LT_ITEM_0100.

    READ TABLE GT_0170 INTO GS_0170 WITH KEY PBARCODE = LS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
                                                                         BINARY SEARCH.
    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      LS_HEAD_0100-CHARG = GS_0170-CHARG.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE GT_5040 INTO GS_5040 WITH KEY PBARCODE = LS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
                                                                         BINARY SEARCH.
    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      LS_HEAD_0100-CHARG = GS_5040-CHARG.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE GT_5031 INTO GS_5031 WITH KEY VBELN = LS_HEAD_0100-VBELN
                                                                         POSNR = LS_HEAD_0100-POSNR
                                                                         PBARCODE = LS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
                                                                         SBARCODE = LS_HEAD_0100-SBARCODE
                                                                         VEHICLE_NO = LS_HEAD_0100-VEHICLE_NO
                                                                         BINARY SEARCH.
    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      LS_HEAD_0100-LFIMG = GS_5031-MENGE.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LS_HEAD_0100 TO LT_ITEM_0100.

*   자재이동
    PERFORM BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE_MOVE USING LT_ITEM_0100
                                                                 CHANGING LT_RETURN
                                                                                  LS_HEAD_0100-RBLNR
                                                                                  LS_HEAD_0100-RJAHR.

*   메세지 처리
    PERFORM SET_MESSAGE USING LT_ITEM_0100
                              LT_RETURN
                     CHANGING LT_EMSG_0100.

    IF LS_HEAD_0100-RBLNR IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM SAVE_PDA_TABLE_M USING LS_HEAD_0100
                                                                 LV_CHK.

      IF LV_CHK = 'E'.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE S000 WITH '데이터 저장 오류.'  DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      ELSE.
        COMMIT WORK.
        DELETE GT_DATA_0100 WHERE PBARCODE EQ LS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

* 메세지 설정
  LOOP AT LT_EMSG_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    MODIFY GT_DATA_0100 FROM LS_DATA_0100
      TRANSPORTING STATS GIMSG MSGTB WHERE PBARCODE EQ LS_DATA_0100-PBARCODE.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM REFRESH_GRID USING GV_GRID_0100
                             GS_LAYOUT.

  IF LT_EMSG_0100 IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE S000 WITH '자재 이동 중 오류 발생.'
      DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE S000 WITH '자재 이동 완료.'.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM EXIT_PROCESS_0700  USING SPACE.

ENDFORM. " GOODS_ISSUE_MOVE

FORM SAVE_PDA_TABLE USING PS_HEAD_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100
                                                  PV_CHK.

  DATA : LS_0170 LIKE ZPPT0170,
             LT_0170 LIKE TABLE OF LS_0170,
             LS_5030 LIKE ZSDT5030,
             LT_5030 LIKE TABLE OF LS_5030,
             LS_5040 LIKE ZSDT5040,
             LT_5040 LIKE TABLE OF LS_5040.

  DATA : LV_TABIX TYPE SY-TABIX.

  CLEAR : GT_PBAR, GS_PBAR.

  SELECT B~VBELN B~POSNR A~PBARCODE A~WERKS B~ZSTATS A~LGORTN
               B~MOVE_TYPE B~LFIMG INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE GT_PBAR
      FROM ZSDT5031 AS A INNER JOIN ZSDT5030 AS B
          ON A~VBELN EQ B~VBELN
        AND A~POSNR EQ B~POSNR
    WHERE B~MOVE_TYPE EQ 'GI' "공장 출하
        AND B~VBELN          EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

  IF GT_PBAR IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_0170
       FROM ZPPT0170
         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN GT_PBAR
     WHERE STOCKSTATE EQ 'B' "재고상태 : 입고
          AND PBARCODE EQ GT_PBAR-PBARCODE.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR LV_TABIX.
  LOOP AT LT_0170 INTO LS_0170.
    LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
    LS_0170-STOCKSTATE = 'D'. "재고상태 : 출고
    LS_0170-CHANGED_DATE = SY-DATUM.
    LS_0170-CHANGED_TIME = SY-UZEIT.
    LS_0170-CHANGED_BY = SY-UNAME.
    MODIFY LT_0170 FROM LS_0170 INDEX LV_TABIX.
  ENDLOOP.

  MODIFY ZPPT0170 FROM TABLE LT_0170.
  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    PV_CHK = 'E'.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR PV_CHK.
  ENDIF.

  IF PV_CHK EQ 'E'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

** ZSDT5040 MODIFY
  IF GT_PBAR IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_5040
       FROM ZSDT5040
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN GT_PBAR
     WHERE PBARCODE EQ GT_PBAR-PBARCODE
          AND STATUS EQ '10'. "소분
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR LV_TABIX.
  LOOP AT LT_5040 INTO LS_5040.
    LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
    LS_5040-STATUS = '20'. "출하
    LS_5040-UPDAT = SY-DATUM.
    LS_5040-UPTIM = SY-UZEIT.
    LS_5040-UPNAM = SY-UNAME.
    MODIFY LT_5040 FROM LS_5040 INDEX LV_TABIX.
  ENDLOOP.

  MODIFY ZSDT5040 FROM TABLE LT_5040.
  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    PV_CHK = 'E'.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR PV_CHK.
  ENDIF.

  IF PV_CHK EQ 'E'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

** ZSDT5030 MODIFY
  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_5030
     FROM ZSDT5030
   WHERE VBELN EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN
        AND MOVE_TYPE EQ 'GI' "공장 출하
        AND ZSTATS IN ('20', '99'). "확정, 취소

  CLEAR LV_TABIX.
  LOOP AT LT_5030 INTO LS_5030.
    LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
    LS_5030-ZSTATS = '30'. "SAP 완료
    LS_5030-UPDAT = SY-DATUM.
    LS_5030-UPTIM = SY-UZEIT.
    LS_5030-UPNAM = SY-UNAME.
    MODIFY LT_5030 FROM LS_5030 INDEX LV_TABIX.
  ENDLOOP.

  MODIFY ZSDT5030 FROM TABLE LT_5030.
  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    PV_CHK = 'E'.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR PV_CHK.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " SAVE_PDATABLE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE_MOVE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LT_ITEM_0100  text
*      -->P_LV_LGORT  text
*      <--P_LT_RETURN  text
*      <--P_LS_HEAD_0100_RBLNR  text
*      <--P_LS_HEAD_0100_RJAHR  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE_MOVE USING PT_DATA_0100 LIKE GT_DATA_0100
                                                         CHANGING PT_RETURN    TYPE BAPIRET2_T
                                                                          P_BELNR
                                                                          P_MJAHR.

  DATA: LT_GM_ITEM    TYPE BAPI2017_GM_ITEM_CREATE_T,     " 아이템
        LS_GM_ITEM    LIKE LINE OF LT_GM_ITEM,
        LS_GM_HEAD_01 LIKE BAPI2017_GM_HEAD_01,           " 헤더
        LS_DATA_0100  LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_RETURN     TYPE BAPIRET2.

  CLEAR: PT_RETURN ,PT_RETURN[], P_BELNR, P_MJAHR.

  READ TABLE PT_DATA_0100 INDEX 1 INTO LS_DATA_0100.

* 헤더
  LS_GM_HEAD_01-PSTNG_DATE = GV_DATE.
  LS_GM_HEAD_01-DOC_DATE = GV_DATE.
  LS_GM_HEAD_01-HEADER_TXT = LS_DATA_0100-PBARCODE.
* 아이템
  LOOP AT PT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    CLEAR: LS_GM_ITEM.
    LS_GM_ITEM-MATERIAL = LS_DATA_0100-MATNR.
    LS_GM_ITEM-PLANT = LS_DATA_0100-PWERKS. "팔레트플랜트
    LS_GM_ITEM-STGE_LOC = LS_DATA_0100-PLGORTN. "팔레트 저장위치
    LS_GM_ITEM-BATCH = LS_DATA_0100-CHARG.
*  이동창고
    LS_GM_ITEM-MOVE_PLANT = LS_DATA_0100-WERKS. "납품문서 플랜트
    LS_GM_ITEM-MOVE_STLOC = LS_DATA_0100-LGORT. " 납품문서 저장위치
* 플랜트 일치 여부에 따라 이동유형 변경
    IF LS_DATA_0100-WERKS EQ LS_DATA_0100-PWERKS.
      LS_GM_ITEM-MOVE_TYPE = '311'.
    ELSE.
      LS_GM_ITEM-MOVE_TYPE = '301'.
    ENDIF.
    LS_GM_ITEM-ENTRY_QNT = LS_DATA_0100-LFIMG.

    IF LS_DATA_0100-AUART EQ 'ZOR' AND LS_DATA_0100-PSTYV EQ 'ZTE'.
      LS_GM_ITEM-SPEC_STOCK     = 'E'.
      LS_GM_ITEM-SALES_ORD      = LS_DATA_0100-VBELV.
      LS_GM_ITEM-S_ORD_ITEM     = LS_DATA_0100-POSNV.
      LS_GM_ITEM-VAL_SALES_ORD  = LS_DATA_0100-VBELV.
      LS_GM_ITEM-VAL_S_ORD_ITEM = LS_DATA_0100-POSNV.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LS_GM_ITEM TO LT_GM_ITEM.
  ENDLOOP.

  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE'
    EXPORTING
      GOODSMVT_HEADER  = LS_GM_HEAD_01
      GOODSMVT_CODE    = '04'
    IMPORTING
      MATERIALDOCUMENT = P_BELNR
      MATDOCUMENTYEAR  = P_MJAHR
    TABLES
      GOODSMVT_ITEM    = LT_GM_ITEM
      RETURN           = PT_RETURN.

  IF P_BELNR IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      EXPORTING
        WAIT = 'X'.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE_MOVE

                                                      PV_CHK TYPE C.

             LS_5040 LIKE ZSDT5040,
             LT_5040 LIKE TABLE OF LS_5040.

  DATA : LV_TABIX TYPE SY-TABIX.

  CLEAR : GT_PBAR, GS_PBAR.

  CLEAR : LS_0170, LS_5031, LS_5040, LT_0170, LT_5031, LT_5040, LS_0170_D, LT_0170_D.

*  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_0170
*     FROM ZPPT0170
*   WHERE FLAG NE 'D'
*        AND STOCKSTATE EQ 'B'
*        AND PBARCODE  EQ PS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE.
** S) 2026.09.01 NYG WERKS Key로 인한 Insert 오류 개선
  LOOP AT GT_0170 INTO GS_0170 WHERE PBARCODE EQ PS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE.

    GS_0170-WERKS = PS_HEAD_0100-WERKS.
    GS_0170-LGORTN = PS_HEAD_0100-LGORT.

  "추가

  ENDIF.

  IF LT_0170 IS NOT INITIAL.
    MODIFY ZPPT0170 FROM TABLE LT_0170.
    IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
      PV_CHK = 'E'.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR PV_CHK.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
** E) 2026.09.01 NYG WERKS Key로 인한 Insert 오류 개선
  IF PV_CHK EQ 'E'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.
*  IF LT_0170 IS NOT INITIAL.
*    CLEAR LV_TABIX.
*    LOOP AT LT_0170 INTO LS_0170.
*      LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
*      LS_0170-WERKS = PS_HEAD_0100-WERKS.

*    ENDLOOP.
