*  ENDIF.

** ZSDT5040 MODIFY
*  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_5040
*     FROM ZSDT5040
*   WHERE PBARCODE EQ PS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE
*        AND STATUS IN ('10', '99'). "소분, 취소
*
*  IF LT_5040 IS NOT INITIAL.
*    CLEAR LV_TABIX.
*    LOOP AT LT_5040 INTO LS_5040.
*      LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
*      LS_5040-WERKS  = PS_HEAD_0100-WERKS.
*      LS_5040-LGORTN = PS_HEAD_0100-LGORT.
*      LS_5040-UPDAT = SY-DATUM.
*      LS_5040-UPTIM = SY-UZEIT.
*      LS_5040-UPNAM = SY-UNAME.
*      MODIFY LT_5040 FROM LS_5040 INDEX LV_TABIX.
*    ENDLOOP.
*
*    MODIFY ZSDT5040 FROM TABLE LT_5040.
*  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT GT_5040 INTO GS_5040 WHERE PBARCODE EQ PS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE.
    GS_5040-WERKS  = PS_HEAD_0100-WERKS.
    GS_5040-LGORTN = PS_HEAD_0100-LGORT.
    GS_5040-UPDAT = SY-DATUM.
    GS_5040-UPTIM = SY-UZEIT.
    GS_5040-UPNAM = SY-UNAME.
    APPEND GS_5040 TO LT_5040.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF LT_5040 IS NOT INITIAL.
    MODIFY ZSDT5040 FROM TABLE LT_5040.
    IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
      PV_CHK = 'E'.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR PV_CHK.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF PV_CHK EQ 'E'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

** ZSDT5031 MODIFY
*  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_5031
*      FROM ZSDT5031
*    WHERE VBELN          EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN
*        AND POSNR         EQ PS_HEAD_0100-POSNR
*        AND PBARCODE   EQ PS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE.
*
*  IF LT_5031 IS NOT INITIAL.
*    LOOP AT LT_5031 INTO LS_5031.
*      LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
*      LS_5031-WERKS  = PS_HEAD_0100-WERKS.
*      LS_5031-LGORTN = PS_HEAD_0100-LGORT.
*      LS_5031-UPDAT = SY-DATUM.
*      LS_5031-UPTIM = SY-UZEIT.
*      LS_5031-UPNAM = SY-UNAME.
*      MODIFY LT_5031 FROM LS_5031 INDEX LV_TABIX.
*    ENDLOOP.
*
*    MODIFY ZSDT5031 FROM TABLE LT_5031.
*  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT GT_5031 INTO GS_5031 WHERE VBELN EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN
                                                        AND POSNR EQ PS_HEAD_0100-POSNR
                                                        AND PBARCODE EQ PS_HEAD_0100-PBARCODE.
    GS_5031-WERKS  = PS_HEAD_0100-WERKS.
    GS_5031-LGORTN = PS_HEAD_0100-LGORT.
    GS_5031-UPDAT = SY-DATUM.
    GS_5031-UPTIM = SY-UZEIT.
    GS_5031-UPNAM = SY-UNAME.
    APPEND GS_5031 TO LT_5031.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF LT_5031 IS NOT INITIAL.
    MODIFY ZSDT5031 FROM TABLE LT_5031.
    IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
      PV_CHK = 'E'.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR PV_CHK.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " SAVE_PDA_TABLE_M
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PRINT_0100_6
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LV_TABIX  text
*      -->P_LV_ROWCNT  text
*      -->P_LT_ZSDS0020  text
*      -->P_LT_ZSDS0021  text
*      -->P_LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS  text
*      -->P_LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM PRINT_0100_6 USING PV_TABINX
                            PV_ROWCNT
                            PT_ZSDS0020           TYPE ZSDTY0020
                            PT_ZSDS0021           TYPE ZSDTY0021
                            PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS     TYPE SSFCOMPOP
                            PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS TYPE SSFCTRLOP.


  DATA: LT_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDTY0021,
         LS_ZSDS0021   TYPE ZSDS0021,
         LV_FM_NAME    TYPE RS38L_FNAM,
         LV_TOTCNT     TYPE INT4,
         LV_FRIDX      TYPE INT4,
         LV_TOIDX      TYPE INT4,
         LV_TIMES      TYPE DEC5_2,
         LV_MOD        TYPE INT4,
         LV_INDEX      TYPE INT4.

* 스마트폼 호출 1페이지에 12행
  LV_TOTCNT = LINES( PT_ZSDS0021 ).
* 12건씩 출력.
  LV_TIMES = TRUNC( LV_TOTCNT / 12 ).
* 나머지
  LV_MOD   = LV_TOTCNT MOD 12.
* 나머지가 있으면 나머지 전송하기 위해 1을 더한다.
  IF LV_MOD GT 0.
    ADD 1 TO LV_TIMES.
  ENDIF.
* From Index
  LV_FRIDX = 1.
* 12건씩 출력.
  DO LV_TIMES TIMES.
    CLEAR: LT_ZSDS0021.
    ADD 1 TO LV_INDEX.
*   미리보기 설정
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
*   첫페이지
    IF PV_TABINX EQ 1 AND LV_INDEX EQ 1.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = ''.
    ENDIF.

*   To Index
    ADD 12 TO LV_TOIDX.
    IF LV_TOIDX GT LV_TOTCNT.
      LV_TOIDX = LV_TOTCNT.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND LINES OF PT_ZSDS0021 FROM LV_FRIDX TO LV_TOIDX TO LT_ZSDS0021
    .
    ADD 12 TO LV_FRIDX.

**   출고의뢰서 %%%%
*    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_1'
*                                  CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
*
*    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
*      EXPORTING
*        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
*        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
*      TABLES
*        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
*        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
*      EXCEPTIONS
*        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
*        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
*        SEND_ERROR         = 3
*        USER_CANCELED      = 4
*        OTHERS             = 5.
*
*    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
*      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
*    ENDIF.
**   반출증
*    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_4'
*                                  CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
*
*    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = 'X'.
*    PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN  = 'X'.
*
**%%%%%%%%%%%
*
*    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
*      EXPORTING
*        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
*        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
*      TABLES
*        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
*        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
*      EXCEPTIONS
*        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
*        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
*        SEND_ERROR         = 3
*        USER_CANCELED      = 4
*        OTHERS             = 5.
*
*    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
*      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
*    ENDIF.

*   반출증 CMTR + 출고서 2장
    PERFORM SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME USING 'ZSDSF0020_8'
                                  CHANGING LV_FM_NAME.
*   마지막페이지
    IF PV_TABINX EQ PV_ROWCNT AND LV_INDEX EQ LV_TIMES.
      PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE = ''.
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION LV_FM_NAME
      EXPORTING
        CONTROL_PARAMETERS = PS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS
        OUTPUT_OPTIONS     = PS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS
      TABLES
        T_HEADER           = PT_ZSDS0020
        T_ITEM             = LT_ZSDS0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        FORMATTING_ERROR   = 1
        INTERNAL_ERROR     = 2
        SEND_ERROR         = 3
        USER_CANCELED      = 4
        OTHERS             = 5.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.

  ENDDO.

ENDFORM. " PRINT_0100_6
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_ZSDT0021
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LS_HEAD_0100  text
*      -->P_SPACE  text
*      -->P_SPACE  text
*      -->P_SPACE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM SAVE_ZSDT0021 USING PS_HEAD_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100
                                              PT_DATA_0100 LIKE GT_DATA_0100
                                              P_CMBLNR
                                              P_CMJAHR
                                              P_CBUDAT.

  DATA: LS_ZSDT0021 LIKE ZSDT0021,
            LT_ZSDT0021 LIKE TABLE OF ZSDT0021.

  DATA : LV_TABIX TYPE SY-TABIX.

  DATA: LS_DATA_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.

  CLEAR LV_TABIX.

  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_ZSDT0021
    FROM ZSDT0021
   WHERE MBLNR EQ PS_HEAD_0100-RBLNR
     AND MJAHR EQ PS_HEAD_0100-RJAHR.

  IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
    LOOP AT LT_ZSDT0021 INTO LS_ZSDT0021.
      LV_TABIX = SY-TABIX.
      LS_ZSDT0021-CMBLNR = P_CMBLNR.
      LS_ZSDT0021-CMJAHR = P_CMJAHR.
      LS_ZSDT0021-CBUDAT = P_CBUDAT.
      LS_ZSDT0021-AENAM = SY-UNAME.
      LS_ZSDT0021-AEDAT = SY-DATUM.
      LS_ZSDT0021-AEZET = SY-UZEIT.
      MODIFY LT_ZSDT0021 FROM LS_ZSDT0021 INDEX LV_TABIX.
    ENDLOOP.
*    CLEAR: ls_zsdt0020-berot.
  ELSE.
    LOOP AT PT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100
       WHERE VBELN EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.
      LS_ZSDT0021-MBLNR = PS_HEAD_0100-RBLNR.
      LS_ZSDT0021-MJAHR = PS_HEAD_0100-RJAHR.
      LS_ZSDT0021-BUDAT = PS_HEAD_0100-RUDAT.
      LS_ZSDT0021-VBELN = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
      LS_ZSDT0021-POSNR = LS_DATA_0100-POSNR.
      LS_ZSDT0021-LGORT = LS_DATA_0100-LGORT.
      LS_ZSDT0021-ERNAM = SY-UNAME.
      LS_ZSDT0021-ERDAT = SY-DATUM.
      LS_ZSDT0021-ERZET = SY-UZEIT.
      LS_ZSDT0021-AENAM = LS_ZSDT0021-ERNAM.
      LS_ZSDT0021-AEDAT = LS_ZSDT0021-ERDAT.
      LS_ZSDT0021-AEZET = LS_ZSDT0021-ERZET.
      APPEND LS_ZSDT0021 TO LT_ZSDT0021.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  MODIFY ZSDT0021 FROM TABLE LT_ZSDT0021.
  COMMIT WORK.

ENDFORM. " SAVE_ZSDT0021
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BAPI_OUTB_DELIVERY_CHANGE_2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LS_DATA_0100_RUDAT  text
*      -->P_LT_ZSDT0021  text
*      -->P_LT_ITEM_0100  text
*      <--P_LT_RETURN  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM BAPI_OUTB_DELIVERY_CHANGE_2 TABLES PT_ZSDT0021 STRUCTURE ZSDT0021
                                                               USING P_LFDAT
                                                               PT_DATA_0100 LIKE GT_DATA_0100
                                                              CHANGING PT_RETURN    TYPE BAPIRET2_T.

  DATA: LS_DATA_0100        LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100,
        LS_HEADER_DATA      LIKE BAPIOBDLVHDRCHG,
        LS_HEADER_CONTROL   LIKE BAPIOBDLVHDRCTRLCHG,
        LS_TECHN_CONTROL    LIKE BAPIDLVCONTROL,
        LT_HEADER_DEADLINES TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF BAPIDLVDEADLN,
        LS_HEADER_DEADLINES LIKE LINE OF LT_HEADER_DEADLINES,
        LT_ITEM_DATA        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF BAPIOBDLVITEMCHG,
        LS_ITEM_DATA        LIKE LINE OF LT_ITEM_DATA,
        LT_ITEM_CONTROL     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF BAPIOBDLVITEMCTRLCHG,
        LS_ITEM_CONTROL     LIKE LINE OF LT_ITEM_CONTROL,
        LT_ITEM_DATA_SPL    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /SPE/BAPIOBDLVITEMCHG
        ,
        LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL    LIKE LINE OF LT_ITEM_DATA_SPL,
        LV_TIMESTAMP_UTC    TYPE CHAR15.

  DATA : PS_ZSDT0021 LIKE ZSDT0021.

  CLEAR: PT_RETURN , PT_RETURN[].

  READ TABLE PT_DATA_0100 INDEX 1 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
* 헤더생성.
  LS_HEADER_DATA-DELIV_NUMB = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
  LS_HEADER_CONTROL-DELIV_NUMB = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
  LS_TECHN_CONTROL-UPD_IND = 'U'.

  LS_HEADER_DEADLINES-DELIV_NUMB = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
  LS_HEADER_DEADLINES-TIMETYPE = 'WSHDRLFDAT'.
  CONCATENATE P_LFDAT '000000'
         INTO LV_TIMESTAMP_UTC.
  LS_HEADER_DEADLINES-TIMESTAMP_UTC = LV_TIMESTAMP_UTC.
  LS_HEADER_DEADLINES-TIMEZONE = 'UTC+9'.
  APPEND LS_HEADER_DEADLINES TO LT_HEADER_DEADLINES.

  LOOP AT PT_DATA_0100 INTO LS_DATA_0100.
    CLEAR: LS_ITEM_DATA, LS_ITEM_CONTROL, LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-DELIV_NUMB = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-DELIV_ITEM = LS_DATA_0100-POSNR.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-MATERIAL = LS_DATA_0100-MATNR.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-FACT_UNIT_NOM = '1'.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-FACT_UNIT_DENOM = '1'.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-BASE_UOM = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
    LS_ITEM_DATA-SALES_UNIT = LS_DATA_0100-MEINS.
    APPEND LS_ITEM_DATA TO LT_ITEM_DATA.

    LS_ITEM_CONTROL-DELIV_NUMB = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
    LS_ITEM_CONTROL-DELIV_ITEM = LS_DATA_0100-POSNR.
    APPEND LS_ITEM_CONTROL TO LT_ITEM_CONTROL.

    LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL-DELIV_NUMB = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN.
    LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL-DELIV_ITEM = LS_DATA_0100-POSNR.
    LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL-PICK_DENIAL = 'X'.
*S_20140107 주석
*   IF gv_save_ok = 'REVC'."출하취소

*      ls_item_data_spl-stge_loc = ls_data_0100-lgort.
*    ELSE.
*    ENDIF.
*E_20140107
** S) 2026.06.06 저장위치 라인 변경
*    LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL-STGE_LOC = P_LGORT.
    READ TABLE PT_ZSDT0021 WITH KEY VBELN = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
                                                            POSNR = LS_DATA_0100-POSNR.
    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL-STGE_LOC = PT_ZSDT0021-LGORT.
    ENDIF.
** E) 2026.06.06 저장위치 라인 변경
    APPEND LS_ITEM_DATA_SPL TO LT_ITEM_DATA_SPL.

  ENDLOOP.

  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_OUTB_DELIVERY_CHANGE'
    EXPORTING
      HEADER_DATA      = LS_HEADER_DATA
      HEADER_CONTROL   = LS_HEADER_CONTROL
      DELIVERY         = LS_DATA_0100-VBELN
      TECHN_CONTROL    = LS_TECHN_CONTROL
    TABLES
      HEADER_DEADLINES = LT_HEADER_DEADLINES
      ITEM_DATA        = LT_ITEM_DATA
      ITEM_CONTROL     = LT_ITEM_CONTROL
      RETURN           = PT_RETURN
      ITEM_DATA_SPL    = LT_ITEM_DATA_SPL.

  READ TABLE PT_RETURN WITH KEY TYPE = 'E'
    TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.

  IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      EXPORTING
        WAIT = 'X'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " BAPI_OUTB_DELIVERY_CHANGE_2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_ZQMT0200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LS_HEAD_0100  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM SAVE_ZQMT0200 USING PS_HEAD_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.

  DATA : LS_0170 LIKE ZPPT0170,
             LT_0170 LIKE TABLE OF LS_0170.

  DATA : BEGIN OF LS_0170_C,
             PBARCODE LIKE ZPPT0170-PBARCODE,
             QCHARG LIKE ZPPT0170-QCHARG,
             REMNG  LIKE ZPPT0170-REMNG,
             END OF LS_0170_C.

  DATA : LT_0170_C LIKE TABLE OF LS_0170_C.

  DATA : BEGIN OF LS_PBAR,
             VBELN         LIKE ZSDT5030-VBELN,
             POSNR        LIKE ZSDT5030-POSNR,
             PBARCODE   LIKE ZPPT0170-PBARCODE,
             SBARCODE   LIKE ZSDT5031-SBARCODE,
             VEHICLE_NO LIKE ZSDT5031-VEHICLE_NO,
             MATNR         LIKE ZSDT5031-MATNR,
             MENGE         LIKE ZSDT5031-MENGE,
             MEINS          LIKE ZSDT5031-MEINS,
             QCHARG       LIKE ZSDT5031-QCHARG,
             WERKS         LIKE ZPPT0170-WERKS,
             ZSTATS        LIKE ZSDT5030-ZSTATS,
             ZSTATS_TXT LIKE DD07T-DDTEXT,
             LGORTN         LIKE ZPPT0170-LGORTN,
             END OF LS_PBAR.

  DATA : LT_PBAR LIKE TABLE OF LS_PBAR.

  DATA : LS_5040 LIKE ZSDT5040,
             LT_5040 LIKE TABLE OF LS_5040.

  DATA : LV_TABIX TYPE SY-TABIX.

  DATA : LV_WERKS TYPE C LENGTH 2.

  CLEAR : LS_PBAR, LT_PBAR, GS_0200, GT_0200.

  SELECT B~VBELN B~POSNR A~PBARCODE A~WERKS B~ZSTATS A~LGORTN
               A~MATNR A~MENGE A~MEINS A~QCHARG
       INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_PBAR
      FROM ZSDT5031 AS A INNER JOIN ZSDT5030 AS B
          ON A~VBELN EQ B~VBELN
        AND A~POSNR EQ B~POSNR
    WHERE B~MOVE_TYPE EQ 'GI' "공장 출하
        AND B~VBELN          EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

  LOOP AT LT_PBAR INTO LS_PBAR.
    CLEAR LV_WERKS.
    GS_0200-KUNNR = PS_HEAD_0100-KUNNR.
    GS_0200-VKBUR = PS_HEAD_0100-VKBUR.
    GS_0200-NAME1 = PS_HEAD_0100-NAME1.
    GS_0200-MATNR = LS_PBAR-MATNR.
    GS_0200-LOTNO = LS_PBAR-QCHARG.
    GS_0200-VBELN = LS_PBAR-VBELN.
    GS_0200-VBELNNO = LS_PBAR-POSNR.
    GS_0200-GIDATE = GV_DATE.
    GS_0200-BEZEI = PS_HEAD_0100-VKBTX.
    GS_0200-KUNN2 = PS_HEAD_0100-YUNNR.
    GS_0200-NAME2 = PS_HEAD_0100-YNAME.
    GS_0200-UNIT = LS_PBAR-MEINS.
    GS_0200-QUANTITY =  LS_PBAR-MENGE.
    GS_0200-ERDAT = SY-DATUM.
    GS_0200-ERZET = SY-UZEIT.
*   품명
    PERFORM GET_MATNR_TEXT USING GS_0200-MATNR
                                           CHANGING GS_0200-MAKTX.

* MTNUM 임의로 생성
    IF LS_PBAR-WERKS EQ '1010'.
      LV_WERKS = 'P1'.
    ELSEIF LS_PBAR-WERKS EQ '1020'.
      LV_WERKS = 'P2'.
    ELSE.
      LV_WERKS = 'P3'.
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION 'NUMBER_GET_NEXT'
      EXPORTING
        NR_RANGE_NR = '01'
        OBJECT      = 'ZQMNO3'
      IMPORTING
        NUMBER      = GS_0200-MTNUM.

    IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
      CONCATENATE LV_WERKS SY-DATUM+2(4) GS_0200-MTNUM
      INTO GS_0200-MTNUM.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND GS_0200 TO GT_0200.
    CLEAR GS_0200.
  ENDLOOP.

  MODIFY ZQMT0200 FROM TABLE GT_0200.

ENDFORM. " SAVE_ZQMT0200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DELETE_ZQMT0200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LS_HEAD_0100  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM DELETE_ZQMT0200 USING PS_HEAD_0100 LIKE LINE OF GT_DATA_0100.

  DATA : LS_QMT0200 LIKE ZQMT0200,
             LT_QMT0200 LIKE TABLE OF LS_QMT0200.

  SELECT * INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE LT_QMT0200
     FROM ZQMT0200
   WHERE VBELN EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.

  IF LT_QMT0200 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE FROM ZQMT0200 WHERE VBELN EQ PS_HEAD_0100-VBELN.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " DELETE_ZQMT0200
